Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a geology algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 569. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 196/4.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_569(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 569. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^196(S, F) is trivial. QED.
